## 01.11.2026 — narrow RU/EN centered labels

**Real defect:** `ThaumcraftReadableText.centeredLabel` appended the three-dot suffix unconditionally after `font.plainSubstrByWidth`. For a button narrower than the suffix itself, the empty prefix still produced `"..."`, rendering outside the allotted rectangle. The code had no early return for zero/negative available width. This is a real clipping/layout error, not a claim about missing translation data.

**Implementation:**
- `src/main/java/dev/ehtid/thaumcraft/port/core/ReadableTextLayout.java`: deterministic code-point-aware binary search using the **active font's pixel width**; fallback from three to two to one dot, or empty string if even one dot cannot fit; avoids splitting supplementary Unicode surrogate pairs. Does not claim full grapheme-cluster segmentation.
- `src/neoforge/java/dev/ehtid/thaumcraft/client/ThaumcraftReadableText.java`: use the shared layout helper for centered labels; skip draw when no pixels available.
- `src/test/java/dev/ehtid/thaumcraft/port/core/ReadableTextLayoutTest.java` and `tools/test_readable_text_layout.sh`: independent Java 21 regression for Cyrillic, ASCII, supplementary emoji and widths -1..150.

**Actual validation:** local Java 21 `bash tools/test_readable_text_layout.sh` — **PASS, 1989 assertions**. [GitHub Actions #41255716435](https://github.com/craftkot/tgaumcraft/actions/runs/41255716435) — SUCCESS on `9703379` (core, full NeoForge build/JAR, dedicated-server smoke). Real Minecraft GUI/client smoke was **not** run.

**Audit:** fixed rubric stays **59/100** and M16 stays **4/6**. This is one localized GUI fix, not complete visuals/audio/localization or full in-game acceptance.

## 01.11.2026 — wrapped multiline overflow, styled final-line ellipsis

**Real defect:** `ThaumcraftReadableText.wrapped` drew the entire last visible line and then overpainted `"..."` at its right edge. The result obscured actual glyphs; when `maxWidth < font.width("...")`, the marker started *left* of the allotted rectangle. `maxLines <= 0` and `maxWidth <= 0` had no early exit.

**Implementation:**
- `ReadableTextLayout.longestPrefixWithSuffix` finds the longest prefix that fits *together* with a marker using the rendered pixel width. Descending scan supports style-dependent/non-monotonic advances and returns -1 when even the suffix cannot fit.
- `ThaumcraftReadableText.wrapped` now stops on nonpositive bounds, replaces only the last visible line when more lines remain, and uses a shortened suffix (3/2/1 dots or empty). It builds a `FormattedCharSequence` retaining the original per-code-point `Style`, so bold/colored text is measured and rendered consistently; supplementary Unicode is not split.
- `ReadableTextLayoutTest` adds seven independent regression assertions for prefix fitting, impossible widths, negative glyph counts and non-monotonic styled advances.

**Actual validation:** Java 21 standalone test **PASS, 1996 assertions**. Additional temporary synthetic Minecraft API stubs compiled the actual client class with `javac --release 21` and exercised `wrapped` for **21 widths (0..20)** with styled glyphs and supplementary Unicode: PASS. This is a **stub test**, not a real NeoForge build, Minecraft screenshot or game-world validation.

**Commits:** `8932022743ab03ad4c623589656fb57416c09fe1` (core), `ae35f64eec53591a9df02599b557794836599f33` (client), `31688d971d70f2e8867457f40bc10b5609e4bdd4` (tests). GitHub PR [#4](https://github.com/craftkot/tgaumcraft/pull/4) remains Draft; no merge. CircleCI [#530](https://circleci.com/gh/craftkot/tgaumcraft/530) was PENDING at initial inspection. Full NeoForge/real client checks for this change not yet confirmed.

**Audit:** unchanged **59/100**, M16 **4/6**. One overflow path fixed, but no real GUI proof or full visual/audio/resource parity.

**Next:** inspect CI on the exact latest branch HEAD, fix any real NeoForge compile failure, then obtain real Minecraft client screenshots of narrow and multiline RU/EN text; audit remaining untranslated/placeholder M16 assets without modifying other workstreams.

## 01.11.2026 — centeredLabel: retain styled visual glyphs

**Defect:** `ThaumcraftReadableText.centeredLabel` converted a rich `Component` to `message.getString()` before truncation and rendering. This discards formatting (including bold/color) and the resolved visual ordering for bidirectional text; width could be computed for different glyph advances than the source Component. The old routine's code-point-safe truncation was not enough for styled text.

**Implementation:** `centeredLabel` now consumes `Component.getVisualOrderText()` and measures/renders the same `FormattedCharSequence`. The existing styled ellipsis routine is reused for narrow labels; full labels retain all original styles and visual order. Empty/zero-width labels do not draw. No unrelated screen/registries or other teams' code was changed.

**Durable regression:** new `tools/test_readable_text_client.py` compiles the **actual** `ThaumcraftReadableText.java` and `ReadableTextLayout.java` with minimal test-only Minecraft API stubs under Java 21. Local result **18 assertions PASS**: bold Cyrillic truncation and centering, unchanged bold full label, visual-order permutation, supplementary emoji, zero/one/two-pixel boundaries. Stub font metrics are synthetic: this verifies our code's data flow and bounds, **not** NeoForge ABI, a Minecraft client, actual glyph widths or screenshots.

**Code commit:** `a08deca494cb350e6069a7d1d5c3b3f142f732f9`. Test commit: `07ef01cca7685b093bdd90a9dc8aee7c5bc70ed7`. [PR #4](https://github.com/craftkot/tgaumcraft/pull/4) remains Draft. GitHub Actions #41264118817 / #41264115339 started on test HEAD; final results must be inspected before claiming full compile/server success. CircleCI #533 was pending at last check. Historical rating **59/100**, M16 **4/6**, unchanged.

**Next:** verify exact HEAD GitHub Actions full NeoForge/JAR/server and investigate any failures; obtain a real Minecraft-client RU/EN visual smoke (bold, mixed-style, narrow labels, bidi). Then audit missing original visual/audio resources and choose the next independent M16 gap; do not claim complete asset parity from this fix.

**CI follow-up on code commit `a08deca`:** [GitHub Actions #41264022328](https://github.com/craftkot/tgaumcraft/actions/runs/41264022328) **SUCCESS**: Core parity, full NeoForge build, dedicated-server smoke, JAR upload. ## 01.11.2026 — M16: Unicode grapheme-safe ellipsis (accent/ZWJ/flag)

**Observed code defect:** Previous `ReadableTextLayout.ellipsize` truncated at Unicode code-point boundaries, and `ThaumcraftReadableText.lastLineWithEllipsis` truncated at individual `FormattedCharSequence` glyph boundaries. Both could leave a base Cyrillic letter without its combining accent, a partial joined emoji, or one regional indicator of a flag. This is a genuine layout bug even though it did not split UTF-16 surrogate pairs.

**Implementation:**
- `ReadableTextLayout.java`: Java 21 `BreakIterator.getCharacterInstance(Locale.ROOT)` determines extended grapheme-cluster boundaries; descending pixel-width check selects the longest fitting *complete* prefix, without assuming monotonic kerning.
- `ThaumcraftReadableText.java`: reconstruct visual code points from styled glyphs, map grapheme UTF-16 boundaries back to styled glyph-prefix counts, and only truncate at those boundaries. Preserve the original `Style` of retained glyphs.
- `ReadableTextLayoutTest.java`: regression for Cyrillic combining accent, woman-scientist ZWJ emoji, Russian flag and non-monotonic synthetic width.
- `tools/test_readable_text_client.py`: stub-based tests for styled combining accents and joined emoji, with both narrow and fitting widths.

**Commits:** core `c807a30`, client `226cd63`, core regression `1070c5a`, client regression `258f643`, corrected synthetic ZWJ width `8c155c4`. Local Java 21 reproduction of the grapheme algorithm and seven focused regression cases: **7/7 PASS**. This is a locally reconstructed focused harness, **not** a run of the repository's full test script. GitHub Actions full NeoForge build/JAR/server on latest code was still **IN PROGRESS** when this entry was written; inspect exact run status before declaring success. Real Minecraft GUI/client has **not** been launched for this change.

**Safety/audit:** changes only in this lane's two layout classes and two dedicated tests. `main`, other feature branches, CI and registries untouched. PR #4 stays Draft. Fixed readiness rubric **59/100**, M16 **4/6** (no +1 for isolated layout corrections).

**Next:** inspect latest GitHub Actions, run `tools/test_readable_text_layout.sh` and `tools/test_readable_text_client.py` in a suitable checkout, then obtain actual Minecraft GUI screenshots to verify font shaping and the visual bounds. Continue missing visual/audio parity after the GUI smoke.

**CI follow-up:** [GitHub Actions #41268171341](https://github.com/craftkot/tgaumcraft/actions/runs/41268171341) **SUCCESS** on `1070c5a`: Core parity, NeoForge build/JAR, dedicated-server smoke and JAR upload. The later `258f643` client stub test and `8c155c4` corrected test fixture were not part of that exact run; their workflows were still in progress. Separate local Java 21 reconstruction of the styled-grapheme prefix mapping passed **6/6** targeted cases. This is algorithm-level evidence, not a run of the actual Minecraft client.


## 01.11.2026 — account for Minecraft's one-pixel font shadow (M16)

**Rendering defect:** both `centeredLabel` and `wrapped` used `font.width(...)` as the entire drawable rectangle, yet called `GuiGraphics.drawString(..., true)`. Minecraft's shadow pass adds one pixel at the right and bottom. A glyph run whose measured width exactly equaled the panel width therefore still drew one pixel beyond its right boundary. The previous regression checked glyph widths, not the shadow footprint.

**Actual source fix:** `src/neoforge/java/dev/ehtid/thaumcraft/client/ThaumcraftReadableText.java` reserves one pixel before truncating centered labels and before splitting/wrapping text. Widths 0–1 skip drawing. Original font size, colors, glyph order, style and gameplay logic are unchanged.

**Durable regression:** `tools/test_readable_text_client.py` now records whether the real helper requested a shadow pass and checks `drawnWidth + 1` against the available width. Boundary tests cover a bold emoji label at exactly 16/17 pixels, one-dot fallback at 2/3 pixels, and a multiline `wrapped` label at 12 pixels. Added a narrowly scoped `.github/workflows/closure-readable-text.yml` to run the existing pure Java pixel/grapheme test and the real GUI-helper compilation against minimal Minecraft API stubs. This workflow is NOT a real Minecraft client and does not replace full NeoForge CI.

**Commits:** `7267ac5` actual client fix; `452f26d` and `843baf6` regression and threshold adjustments; `71e7baa` independent test workflow. A standalone Java 21 synthetic boundary reproduction passed; **do not report the repository stub suite or latest NeoForge CI as passed until the new GitHub Actions runs finish**.

**Safety/audit:** work is confined to this lane's existing GUI helper, dedicated test and new independent workflow. No other feature branch or `main` was modified. Historical audit stays **59/100**, M16 **4/6**. No real Minecraft GUI screenshot or BETA26-wide visual/audio parity proof yet.

**Next:** inspect the exact-head regression workflow and full NeoForge CI, fix any actual failure; then add real client GUI screenshot evidence for narrow RU/EN labels, and move to missing original models/FX/audio after verified GUI acceptance.


**Verification follow-up:** [M16 isolated regression Actions #41271605217](https://github.com/craftkot/tgaumcraft/actions/runs/41271605217) completed **SUCCESS** on `71e7baa`. Actual logs: `Readable text pixel bounds: OK (2003 assertions)` and `Client styled GUI layout stubs: OK (38 assertions)`. Full NeoForge/JAR/server Actions [#41271605240](https://github.com/craftkot/tgaumcraft/actions/runs/41271605240) remained **IN PROGRESS** at inspection. No real-client screenshot; 59/100 and M16 4/6 remain unchanged.


## 01.11.2026 — M16: unsplittable glyph in a narrow wrapped label

**Code defect:** `ThaumcraftReadableText.wrapped` trusted every visible `Font.split` line to fit `maxWidth - 1`, except the final line when more lines were hidden. A single indivisible glyph (e.g. emoji/CJK) can be wider than the split width. With `maxLines=1` and only one returned line, it was drawn unbounded, including the shadow, outside the panel.

**Fix:** `src/neoforge/java/dev/ehtid/thaumcraft/client/ThaumcraftReadableText.java` now passes every ordinary visible line through the existing grapheme-safe, styled, pixel-bounded `fitStyledLine` helper. For the last visible line with additional hidden lines, the existing explicit ellipsis path is unchanged. No changes to global registries, world logic, CI settings or other teams' files.

**Regression:** `tools/test_readable_text_client.py` now exercises the real GUI helper against synthetic Minecraft API stubs with one unsplittable 6px glyph inside a 5px shadow-inclusive box (expected two dots), and a 7px box where the full glyph fits. This test is not a real Minecraft client. Commits: code `21acb388d05b3849ebeb8f7989187052c54e2dda`, test `581920cd4de7ee1c674499707b831928f6b24b72`.

Do not claim either new run passed before checking it.

Covers all 37 source colors, base/other layers, blank and invalid aspects. `verify_label_brace_source_art.py` also checks event wiring. The existing isolated M16 Actions workflow now runs both checks. Full NeoForge and real-client validation must be inspected on the exact new HEAD before claiming success.

## 01.11.2026 — M16: настоящий клиентский арт подтверждён, усилен регрессионный критерий

**Полученные доказательства:** [real Minecraft Actions #41287870087](https://github.com/craftkot/tgaumcraft/actions/runs/41287870087) **SUCCESS** на кодовом SHA `989ce37`. Лог реального Xvfb/Mesa/NeoForge 1.21.1 клиента подтвердил создание интегрированного creative/peaceful мира с разрешёнными командами, четыре фактических hotbar-скриншота, один общий creative-inventory скриншот и `LABEL_REAL_CLIENT_VISUAL_SMOKE=PASS`. На старом тесте сравнения 32×32 были ненулевые AE: brace/blank 372, blank/Aer 303, blank/Ignis 303, Aer/Ignis 68. Реальные PNG из GitHub artifact [#13074984471](https://github.com/craftkot/tgaumcraft/actions/runs/41287870087/artifacts/13074984471) **скачаны и визуально просмотрены**: в inventory видны разные предметы, у Aer зеленоватая заливка, у Ignis оранжево-красная. Это именно скриншоты Minecraft, не макеты.

**Найденный дефект теста:** старый crop с центром `Y+HEIGHT-22` захватывал только нижнюю часть иконки вместе с границей hotbar. Поэтому простой AE>0 мог пройти из-за фонового отличия, не проверяя основной цвет аспекта. Исправлено в [`a81f726`](https://github.com/craftkot/tgaumcraft/commit/a81f726c29192e5bf8bf1a14c940e66498f35bfd): центр crop сдвинут на 20 px вверх, проверены границы реального скриншота, для центральных 16×16 добавлено независимое сравнение `mean.r-mean.g`. Ignis должен иметь существенно более красную заливку, чем Aer; иначе клиентский тест падает. Это не вмешивается в игровую механику и не требует изменений чужих веток.

**Локальная повторная проверка на реальных PNG из #41287870087:** новый полный 32×32 crop содержит всю иконку; для центральных 16×16 Aer `R-G=0.0435968`, Ignis `R-G=0.311857`, проверка порога **PASS**. Это проверка нового алгоритма на сохранённых реальных кадрах, **не** новый полный клиентский запуск на `a81f726`. Новый точный CI необходимо проверить отдельно. Артефакты остаются доступны до 15.11.2026.

**Следующее:** проверить точный клиентский CI на `a81f726`; при падении исправить конкретную ошибку. Затем взять отдельный оригинальный BETA26 звук или FX, которого не хватает в игре, с независимым подтверждением и без пересечений с M06/M07/M10/M04.

## 01.11.2026 — M16: collapsed panel geometry regression

**Observed code defect:** the existing `ThaumcraftReadableText.panel` always called its interior `GuiGraphics.fill` even for a 0px, 1px or 2px panel. At width=1 it submitted `(x+1, y+1) -> (x, y)`, an inverted rectangle; at width=0 even the border was zero-area. Such draw commands are not safe clipping and may paint outside narrow UI bounds. This is a modern GUI-helper bug, not a claim that an original BETA26 gameplay feature was missing.

**Fix:** in the existing M16-owned helper, skip non-positive panel sizes and only draw the inner rectangle when both dimensions exceed 2. Normal panels (3px and larger) keep the exact original two fills and colors. Dedicated Java client-stub regression now records each real helper `fill` call, asserts no inverted/empty rectangles, and checks the 3x3 interior coordinates. No shared registry, sound/FX, Gradle, other lane, or `main` edits.

**Verification status at commit creation:** structural review complete; GitHub Actions must verify the exact commit. The prior branch HEAD `056995d` had real label client #41291945725 SUCCESS, M16 regression #41291945686 SUCCESS, full NeoForge/JAR/server #41291945670 SUCCESS. Those are *baseline* results, not tests of this patch. The prior label in-game evidence covers brace/blank/Aer/Ignis only. **Audit remains 59/100, M16 4/6**; no parity credit for guarding a collapsed rectangle.

**Next:** inspect exact-head M16 Java regression and full NeoForge CI; if green, resume missing original BETA26 FX/audio work with independent asset provenance and real client evidence. PR #4 stays Draft; no merge.

## 01.11.2026 — M16: restore BETA26 entity-height scan rune lifetime

**Source comparison (pinned original):** `TheDarkTower314/Thaumcraft-6-Source-Code@954022bb`, `ItemThaumometer.drawFX` calls `FXDispatcher.blockRunes` with duration units `(int)(target.height * 15)` for entity targets and `15` for block targets. `FXBlockRunes` sets `particleMaxAge = 3 * m`. Its dispatcher adds 0.5 to each incoming position, so the existing modern entity/block anchor coordinates are already correct; **do not offset them again**.

**Defect:** `ScanRuneParticle` hard-coded `lifetime=45` for both target types. The modern entity scan therefore expired too early for tall targets and too late for small ones, unlike BETA26. This is a real visual timing mismatch, not a missing sound file.

**Implementation:** `ThaumometerRuneFx` passes source-derived entity duration units to `ClientLevel.addParticle` (15 for blocks). `ScanRuneParticle.Provider` decodes the otherwise-unused Y-speed slot as duration units, forces original green color channel to zero, and applies `3*m` ticks. `LegacyScanRuneDuration` keeps the exact integer truncation for normal positive entity heights, with guards for zero/invalid/oversized values. No global particle/sound registry, original textures, other feature branches or `main` changed.

**Changes:** `src/neoforge/java/dev/ehtid/thaumcraft/client/ThaumometerRuneFx.java`, `src/neoforge/java/dev/ehtid/thaumcraft/client/ScanRuneParticle.java`, new `src/main/java/dev/ehtid/thaumcraft/port/core/LegacyScanRuneDuration.java`, `src/test/java/dev/ehtid/thaumcraft/port/core/LegacyScanRuneDurationTest.java`, `tools/test_scan_rune_duration.sh`, dedicated `.github/workflows/closure-readable-text.yml` and this lane journal.

**Commits:** core `c0e2116`, test `ea2629f`, harness `4674257`, actual FX `a1d8e5b`, particle `79a9d5f`, isolated CI `e5d0009`. Standalone Java 21 regression **13/13 PASS locally** (`bash tools/test_scan_rune_duration.sh`). This validates timing math only. **Exact-head NeoForge compile, dedicated server and actual Minecraft-client rune timing are not yet confirmed**; inspect the workflows triggered by the code changes. The earlier real label-client #41296376246 is SUCCESS on the *previous* `68e81c9` and does not test the new scan-rune behavior.

**Audit:** fixed baseline remains **59/100**, M16 **4/6**. One scan particle lifetime bug fixed in code; no M16-wide audio/FX acceptance or point increase. PR #4 stays Draft and must not be merged without owner approval.

Its actual job log reports 2003 text-core assertions, 54 GUI-helper stub assertions, 118 label-tint assertions and **13 source-derived scan-rune duration assertions**. ## 01.11.2026 — M16: world-space scan rune orientation (BETA26 source parity)

**Verified original:** pinned `TheDarkTower314/Thaumcraft-6-Source-Code@954022bb`, `FXBlockRunes.renderParticle` applies `glRotatef(rotation, 0,1,0)` and then `glRotatef(90, 0,0,1)`. `rotation` is a random multiple of 90 degrees. The old NeoForge `ScanRuneParticle` instead stored the random turn in `roll`, which rotates a camera-facing billboard in the view plane. Looking around therefore changed the apparent rune plane relative to the world: **not** the BETA26 geometry.

**Fix:** `ScanRuneParticle.getFacingCameraMode()` now supplies the original world-space Y-quarter-turn followed by a Z 90-degree tip via the 1.21.1 `SingleQuadParticle.FacingCameraMode` quaternion. Billboard `roll` is zero; alpha, sprite, duration and gravity are unchanged. No common registries, research/alchemy/infusion/golem files, or `main` modified.

**Code:** [4420341](https://github.com/craftkot/tgaumcraft/commit/4420341ff08b4c2c8ee01fa561dd0dd784a2eafb). **Regression:** [2c61096](https://github.com/craftkot/tgaumcraft/commit/2c61096de475db75b58bfbc50bb6e076e455db70) adds `tools/test_scan_rune_orientation.py`, which compiles the **actual** particle class and core duration helper with Java 21 API stubs, instantiates 128 particles, checks four world-Y quarter turns, exact 90-degree Z tip, no billboard roll, fixed orientation across camera/partial ticks and 45-tick block duration. This is a client-class regression using synthetic API stubs, **not a real rendered screenshot**. [a95ec6e](https://github.com/craftkot/tgaumcraft/commit/a95ec6e96f0da3963e1e713d64a76481f823a2da) wires the test into isolated M16 CI.

**Validation at journal write:** [M16 workflow #41304894278](https://github.com/craftkot/tgaumcraft/actions/runs/41304894278) QUEUED and [full NeoForge #41304894262](https://github.com/craftkot/tgaumcraft/actions/runs/41304894262) QUEUED on test/workflow HEAD `a95ec6e`. The earlier M16 GUI #41304868320 was SUCCESS on code-only `4420341`, but did not include the new orientation regression. **Do not claim exact-head PASS until logs are inspected.** Existing label-client smoke is unrelated to rune world orientation.

**Audit:** fixed historical baseline **59/100**, M16 **4/6**. No +1 without real in-game particle visual acceptance and broader M16 audio/FX coverage. PR #4 remains Draft; no merge.

**Next:** inspect exact-head M16 and NeoForge jobs; repair any compile/test failure first. Then build a genuine in-world scan-rune screenshot/timing probe to distinguish camera-facing billboards from original world-space runes. Continue original sound events after this visual check.

## 01.11.2026 — M16: restore BETA26 scan rune face-space placement

**Verified original:** `TheDarkTower314/Thaumcraft-6-Source-Code@954022bb`, `FXBlockRunes.renderParticle` executes world-Y quarter turn, then local-Z 90-degree rotation, **then** `glTranslated(ofx, ofy, -0.51)`. `ofx` ranges [0,0.2), `ofy` ranges [-0.3,0.3). `FXDispatcher.blockRunes` adds 0.5 to the anchor; `ItemThaumometer.drawFX` passes block coordinates and entity coordinates already compensated by -0.5. The earlier port incorrectly added `ofx` to **world X** and `ofy` to **world Y**, and completely omitted the original -0.51 local-Z displacement. Consequently the rune quads were anchored inside/near the scanned block instead of its four original faces.

**Fix:** existing M16-owned `ScanRuneParticle` transforms the original local displacement using the same four discrete world-Y rotations as its quad: N (dx=-ofy,dz=-.51), W (dx=-.51,dz=ofy), S (dx=ofy,dz=.51), E (dx=.51,dz=-ofy); world-Y offset is `ofx`. Initial previous-position coordinates are synchronized after placement to avoid a first-frame interpolation jump. Sprite, color, original 0.03 gravity, 45/entity-derived lifetime and orientation remain unchanged. No shared registry, other feature lane, `main` or Gradle touched.

**Code:** [b9a8df2](https://github.com/craftkot/tgaumcraft/commit/b9a8df2513346606978c020c4327d851cc6356e6). **Regression:** [4862724](https://github.com/craftkot/tgaumcraft/commit/4862724e901a9a4a66a34402a2c430d0a56e510a) extends the existing Java-21 test of the **actual particle source compiled with Minecraft API stubs**: all four face planes at exactly 0.51 offset, tangent jitter range, vertical local offset, and stable previous position, for 128 particles. Standalone Python mathematical cross-check against the Y*Z rotation formula: 2000/2000 placements PASS. This cross-check is not a NeoForge compile or rendered client test.

**CI at initial journal write:** [exact code+test M16 regression #41309819796](https://github.com/craftkot/tgaumcraft/actions/runs/41309819796) and [full NeoForge/JAR/server #41309819818](https://github.com/craftkot/tgaumcraft/actions/runs/41309819818) were IN PROGRESS. Do not claim their success before checking exact SHA `4862724`. Last prior head `24402e2` passed [M16 regression #41304962669](https://github.com/craftkot/tgaumcraft/actions/runs/41304962669), [full CI #41304962690](https://github.com/craftkot/tgaumcraft/actions/runs/41304962690) and [real label-client #41304962753](https://github.com/craftkot/tgaumcraft/actions/runs/41304962753); none tests new rune placement.

**Audit:** historical 59/100, M16 4/6; no point credited without real scan-rune in-game evidence and broader visual/audio parity. PR #4 remains Draft; no merge. **Next:** inspect exact-head CI and fix any failure, then add a real Minecraft-client scan-rune camera/face/timing smoke rather than treating label screenshots or stub tests as rendered-rune proof. After that, continue missing original sound events with actual gameplay wiring.

Actual job log: **1409 ScanRuneOrientationTest assertions PASS**, now including face-space offsets and first-frame coordinates; 2003 text-core, 54 GUI-stub, 118 tint and 13 duration assertions also PASS. [Full NeoForge #41309819818](https://github.com/craftkot/tgaumcraft/actions/runs/41309819818) was still **IN PROGRESS** at the `NeoForge build` step when checked. ## 01.11.2026 — M16: actual client found and fixed scan-rune velocity drift

**Real failure evidence, not a synthetic test:** [live client #41314614960](https://github.com/craftkot/tgaumcraft/actions/runs/41314614960) (SHA `47f67a8`) created, rendered and expired 40 rune particles with correct initial four-face coordinates and 45-tick lifetimes. **Its actual screenshot `71-rune-during-use.png` shows runes visibly scattered far into the sky instead of remaining at the scanned gold block.** Original pinned `FXBlockRunes` explicitly resets `motionX=motionY=motionZ=0` after construction. The NeoForge `Particle` velocity constructor adds randomized motion even for zero input speed, which the earlier port had not reset. This was a **real gameplay/visual bug** missed by stub-only orientation tests.

**Gameplay fix:** [b5ce2db](https://github.com/craftkot/tgaumcraft/commit/b5ce2dbed08a0c88d4ffceb95ec642659b96351c) explicitly resets `xd/yd/zd` to zero after `super(...,0,0,0)` in `ScanRuneParticle`. Original gravity remains 0.03, so the rune may fall naturally but no longer receives unintended random horizontal motion. The opt-in actual-client expiry marker now reports horizontal drift. [Regression 1d0dc29](https://github.com/craftkot/tgaumcraft/commit/1d0dc29e8923d245c3791d8461d9409b873ff73d) makes the synthetic vanilla constructor deliberately inject nonzero velocity and asserts the **real class** zeroes it and does not drift after ticking. [M16 regression #41315190510](https://github.com/craftkot/tgaumcraft/actions/runs/41315190510) **SUCCESS**, log: **1665 scan-rune assertions**.

**Client smoke blocker and repair:** [#41314620065](https://github.com/craftkot/tgaumcraft/actions/runs/41314620065) **FAILURE**: in that random fresh world, origin chunk was not loaded; vanilla chat said `That position is not loaded` for `/fill` and `/setblock`, leaving an empty-water screenshot and 0 particles. This is a test-fixture/world-preparation failure, not a gameplay defect. [93b1567](https://github.com/craftkot/tgaumcraft/commit/93b1567f2cc04dd39cd1dd3b63507d154b8717d5) now `/forceload`s origin and requires real vanilla block and item predicates before scanning. [#41315090717](https://github.com/craftkot/tgaumcraft/actions/runs/41315090717) **SUCCESS** for that world setup. [88d774b](https://github.com/craftkot/tgaumcraft/commit/88d774bc97dc5c579a642b0e9686ee585504fa06) additionally requires zero horizontal drift from the real particle expiry log.

**Exact latest code+test proof:** [full NeoForge/JAR/dedicated server #41315207341](https://github.com/craftkot/tgaumcraft/actions/runs/41315207341) **SUCCESS** on `88d774bc`; [real Minecraft/Xvfb client #41315207365](https://github.com/craftkot/tgaumcraft/actions/runs/41315207365) **SUCCESS** on the same SHA. ## 01.11.2026 — M16: BETA26 Thaumonomicon page PCM source attribution

**Engineering change:** [commit 5e81df9](https://github.com/craftkot/tgaumcraft/commit/5e81df9c8e54d98236fb783c21eed6e7ebb309c8) adds `tools/match_page_audio_pcm.py`, a source-clip attribution check. It decodes the original two OGGs and raw captured OpenAL/PulseAudio monitor via ffmpeg to 8-kHz mono, trims source silence, computes FFT-based *locally normalized* cross-correlation over the entire event window, and rejects a match below 0.20 or without a 0.08 margin over the baseline. It reports the matched clip, correlation and offset, not merely output loudness. `tools/test_page_audio_match.py` supplies deterministic synthetic positive and noise-only negative controls. The isolated client workflow now runs both checks on real captured PCM after the existing gameplay/audio smoke, uploading the report with game evidence.

**Changed paths:** `tools/match_page_audio_pcm.py`, `tools/test_page_audio_match.py`, `.github/workflows/closure-book-page-client.yml` and this lane journal. No Java gameplay, global sound registry, main, Gradle, or foreign lane files changed. Original OGG Git blobs remain `a0c4f5b0` and `2db59662`.

**Validation boundary:** local reproduction with Python 3 / NumPy / ffmpeg on synthetic Vorbis audio passed: correct page2 selected with normalized correlation 1.0000, wrong page1 0.0371; pure-noise negative control rejected. This is *not* a run against the original BETA26 clips or actual Minecraft PCM. Exact-code GitHub [client waveform #41339861194](https://github.com/craftkot/tgaumcraft/actions/runs/41339861194) and [full NeoForge #41339861175](https://github.com/craftkot/tgaumcraft/actions/runs/41339861175) were **IN PROGRESS** when checked. Do not claim source-waveform parity until their exact-head results and logs are inspected. If source attribution fails, first inspect correlation/offset and whether OpenAL Soft resampling, clipping, noise or timing invalidates the matcher; do not weaken thresholds blindly.

## 02.11.2026 — M16: BETA26 Curio pitch-floor parity, real-client evidence pending

Fresh main HEAD `a4280834`, own starting HEAD `ea879b5`. Read main ROADMAP/AGENTS/parallel workflow/fixed audit, own lane and latest Actions. PR #1–3 full changed-file lists have no overlap with the six paths below. The previously blocked page/scan PCM client runs are now **SUCCESS** on exact prior HEAD `ea879b5`: [page #41355462797](https://github.com/craftkot/tgaumcraft/actions/runs/41355462797), [scan #41355462763](https://github.com/craftkot/tgaumcraft/actions/runs/41355462763), [Curio #41355462752](https://github.com/craftkot/tgaumcraft/actions/runs/41355462752), [full NeoForge #41355462775](https://github.com/craftkot/tgaumcraft/actions/runs/41355462775). No need to weaken source-correlation checks further.

**Root-cause finding:** the previously suspected Curio `pitch=0.5000` defect is **not evidence of lost BETA26 audio parity**. Original Minecraft 1.12.2 `SoundManager#getClampedPitch(ISound)` is `MathHelper.clamp(soundIn.getPitch(), 0.5F, 2.0F)` ([original 1.12 source](https://github.com/tw1nside/MCP-1.12.2-Gradle/blob/master/src/main/java/net/minecraft/client/audio/SoundManager.java)); modern NeoForge 1.21.1 `SoundEngine#calculatePitch` likewise clamps to 0.5..2.0 ([1.21.1 API](https://lexxie.dev/neoforge/1.21.1/net/minecraft/client/sounds/SoundEngine.html)). The exact original ItemCurio random formula requests 1/3..1/2, so **both engines should audibly play at 0.5**, while retaining the original requested RNG formula. Altering pitch to make it audible below 0.5 would actually break BETA26 parity.

**Code/test commit:** [b3d6f1b](https://github.com/craftkot/tgaumcraft/commit/b3d6f1b57f5e09d4afde593c99840ea5638c89e0). `CurioItem.java` emits the requested pitch only in opt-in smoke mode, without changing the original sound or gameplay RNG. New `tools/check_curio_pitch_parity.py` compares that authoritative server value against the original-`learn.ogg` real PCM pitch fitted by the existing matcher, using the **original and modern** clamp expectation. `tools/test_curio_pitch_parity.py` supplies four positive and six negative deterministic controls. Updated `tools/test_curio_learn_sound.py`, `tools/client_curio_learn_sound_smoke.py` and the isolated Curio workflow. No global registry, shared Gradle, main or foreign branch changes.

**Verification boundary:** [new Curio real-client #41361055352](https://github.com/craftkot/tgaumcraft/actions/runs/41361055352) and [full NeoForge #41361055301](https://github.com/craftkot/tgaumcraft/actions/runs/41361055301) started on exact code HEAD `b3d6f1b`; their final outcomes and requested-vs-observed values must be inspected before claiming PASS. Historical fixed audit remains **59/100, M16 4/6**. PR #4 remains Draft, no merge. **Next:** check exact-head client and full CI; if pitch gate fails inspect authoritative server marker and source-PCM fit, then select the next missing independent BETA26 audio/FX event.

Real integrated-server requested pitch **0.35975**, source-matched actual PCM pitch **0.50000**, exactly the `max(0.5, requested)` audible output of both original Minecraft 1.12.2 and NeoForge 1.21.1. ## 02.11.2026 — M16 Salis: BETA26 immutable-AABB source correction

- Re-read main `a4280834adc5a20b6951098af937d30ca4aeca56`, ROADMAP, AGENTS, parallel workflow, fixed 59/100 audit, own lane and PR #1–4. Last previous-head [real Salis #41478217535](https://github.com/craftkot/tgaumcraft/actions/runs/41478217535) SUCCESS (real client); previous [full NeoForge #41478217142](https://github.com/craftkot/tgaumcraft/actions/runs/41478217142) SUCCESS. - **Source-parity defect found in prior M16 work:** original BETA26 `FXDispatcher.drawBlockSparkles` at `TheDarkTower314/Thaumcraft-6-Source-Code@954022bb` calls `bs.grow(0.1, 0.1, 0.1);` **without assigning the returned box**. Minecraft 1.12.2 `AxisAlignedBB.grow` returns a new immutable AABB (1.12.2 Forge Javadocs, `AxisAlignedBB.grow(double,double,double)`); it does NOT modify `bs`. Our previous `inflate(0.1)` introduced an unintended difference and increased the fully exposed cube from 6*40 + 50 = **290** to 6*48 + 50 = **338** individual actor-only particle packets.

## 03.11.2026 — M16 Salis: zero-delay unloaded-chunk dispatch guard

- Re-read fresh main roadmap, AGENTS, parallel workflow, fixed audit and lane; open PR #1–4. No foreign paths touched. ## 06.11.2026 — M16: correct server-side Salis dispatch evidence

**Observed test defect:** `SALIS_SPARKLES_PACKETS_SENT` was emitted at the end of `SalisMundusSparkles.sendToActor` after scheduling FX, not after `ServerLevel.sendParticles`. ## 09.11.2026 — BETA26 crystal selection outline on all six support faces

- Found a concrete M16 gameplay/UI defect: CrystalClusterBlock.getShape always returned the same inset central box, regardless of whether the crystal was attached to a floor, ceiling, wall or several faces. Original BETA26 BlockCrystal.getBoundingBox derives its outline from six independent support flags: exactly one support selects the corresponding half-cube; two or more (and the defensive no-support state) use a full cube. The original collision shape remains empty; this change only corrects the selectable outline.
- Added pure-core LegacyCrystalOutlineBounds and wired the actual NeoForge block getShape to the same six hasCrystalSupport predicates already used by survival and the baked renderer. The existing support predicate itself was not broadened.

Local Java 21 isolated regression: **24/24 assertions PASS**. This is core geometry parity, not a real client screenshot or a full NeoForge build.
- BETA26 reference: original BlockCrystal.getBoundingBox/getExtendedState (decompiled reference: https://github.com/TheDarkTower314/Thaumcraft-6-Source-Code/blob/master/src/main/java/thaumcraft/common/blocks/world/ore/BlockCrystal.java); repository source-JAR fingerprint is recorded in analysis/HANDOFF_CRYSTAL_OBJ_2026_10_13.md.
- Files: src/main/java/dev/ehtid/thaumcraft/port/core/LegacyCrystalOutlineBounds.java (new), src/neoforge/java/dev/ehtid/thaumcraft/block/CrystalClusterBlock.java, src/test/java/dev/ehtid/thaumcraft/port/core/CrystalMeshParityTest.java, this lane journal.

## 09.11.2026 — make neighbor-dependent crystal outlines dynamic

- Inspected the real Minecraft-client artifact from [outline run #42674509786](https://github.com/craftkot/tgaumcraft/actions/runs/42674509786), exact HEAD `337a7cd93904faca4cd50d0f5c86f2114f620dd5`. The Python contract and NeoForge build passed; the actual integrated client created a world and completed the UP miss/hit pair. In the DOWN miss case, the server confirmed the eye-origin probe command and confirmed its 98% probe point was inside `thaumcraft:crystal_aqua`, but after the left click the crystal was gone and `CRYSTAL_OUTLINE_MISS_RETAINED_down` was absent. The uploaded artifact contains the screenshot and authoritative client/server log.
- The original BETA26 `BlockCrystal#getBoundingBox` recomputes support-face flags from the world. The port's `CrystalClusterBlock#getShape` also reads adjacent blocks directly, but its `BlockBehaviour.Properties` were not marked dynamic. NeoForge 1.21.1 exposes `Properties.dynamicShape()`; without this declaration, the world-dependent shape may be cached as if it were position-independent. The UP→DOWN sequence is consistent with reuse of the previous support orientation. This is the production cause being tested, not yet an accepted fix.
- Changed the constructor to pass `props.dynamicShape()` to `Block`, preserving all seven registrations and avoiding the shared registry file. Added a static regression contract so future edits cannot silently remove the dynamic-shape declaration.
- The exact-head full NeoForge CI and server smoke on the preceding SHA succeeded ([push #42674503241](https://github.com/craftkot/tgaumcraft/actions/runs/42674503241), [PR #42674509795](https://github.com/craftkot/tgaumcraft/actions/runs/42674509795)); these do not prove the new behavior. The crystal-outline real-client test failed at the DOWN miss-retained assertion after a real click ([#42674509786](https://github.com/craftkot/tgaumcraft/actions/runs/42674509786)).
- No audit points are added. Fixed score remains 59/100, M16 4/6. Next: require a fresh exact-commit Minecraft-client run to pass all six miss-retained/hit-removed pairs plus UP+EAST multi-face and all 13 screenshots; inspect the artifact and logs before accepting the outline.

## M16 iteration — prefer the server's real block-outline hit point for Salis face delay (2026-11-10)

- Fresh main HEAD: `a4280834adc5a20b6951098af937d30ca4aeca56`; own branch HEAD before this commit: `28901556912efddf8ee933988abd021eddd2d6b7`; PR #4 is still open Draft. The changed-file lists of PR #1–#3 were rechecked: none touches `SalisMundusSparkles.java`, `verify_salis_sparkle_particle_log.py`, `verify_dust_particles.py`, or this lane journal. The only existing PR #3/#4 overlap remains `SalisMundusItem.java`, deliberately untouched.
- BETA26 reference: `ItemMagicDust#doSparkles` receives the actual hit coordinates from `onItemUseFirst` and computes the face-particle delay from that hit point; `FXDispatcher.drawBlockSparkles` uses the same start vector for every exposed-face particle.
- The port previously approximated that point by clipping a full unit-cube AABB from the server player's eye ray. That can diverge from the real outline shape for partial blocks. To avoid the shared `SalisMundusItem.onItemUseFirst` hunk, `SalisMundusSparkles` now re-runs the server player's 1.21.1 outline pick (`player.pick(6.0D, 1.0F, false)`) and accepts its `BlockHitResult` only when it resolves to the clicked `BlockPos`. If server rotation no longer resolves to that block, the prior bounded AABB fallback remains. This is a server-side re-raycast, not the original client packet's exact hit vector; that final limitation remains explicit.
- Added smoke markers for the accepted BlockHitResult and fallback. The real-client log verifier now requires exactly one accepted hitpoint and rejects fallback in the bookshelf scenario. The resource/source guard checks the same production path.
- Changed files: `src/neoforge/java/dev/ehtid/thaumcraft/item/SalisMundusSparkles.java`, `tools/verify_salis_sparkle_particle_log.py`, `tools/verify_dust_particles.py`, and this journal.
- Before this iteration, exact HEAD `28901556912efddf8ee933988abd021eddd2d6b7` had full Core/NeoForge/JAR/dedicated-server CI SUCCESS [#42719413042](https://github.com/craftkot/tgaumcraft/actions/runs/42719413042), real-client Salis hand+face sprites and original dust PCM SUCCESS [#42719413974](https://github.com/craftkot/tgaumcraft/actions/runs/42719413974), and delayed-queue JVM regression SUCCESS [#42719413556](https://github.com/craftkot/tgaumcraft/actions/runs/42719413556). The real client recorded 50 hand particles created/48 rendered/50 expired, 240 face particles created/208 rendered/240 expired, original 16-frame 320/512 animation, and dust PCM correlation 0.4160 against baseline 0.0000. ### 10.11.2026 — исправлен блокер первого real-client tube smoke

- Точный предыдущий HEAD d1ad9c807b4a7c9dd22732804d3295119419fbbc; [PR client run #42749763408](https://github.com/craftkot/tgaumcraft/actions/runs/42749763408) завершился FAILURE на шаге запуска клиента, а не на проверке atlas/model. Python asset guard, offline harness и bash -n прошли. Артефакт 13732454991 и настоящий client.log изучены.
- Доказанная причина первого отказа: в generated Bash осталось буквальное ${metric%% *} из-за лишнего escape в Python raw string. compare -metric AE реально дал 165202 (0.16133), но оболочка не раскрыла переменную; сообщение об ошибке содержало literal metric=${metric%% *}. Это дефект тестового harness, не производственного кода.
- Вторая независимая проблема сценария: первая серия fill/setblock air была отправлена до загрузки chunk и Minecraft ответил That position is not loaded; игрок затем телепортировался на Y=150 без опорной платформы и мог упасть до снимка. Поэтому прежний pixel diff не является доказательством видимости труб, даже при трёх серверных blockstate markers.
- Исправлено в изолированном smoke: force-load chunk (0,0) до платформы; построить пол и подтвердить TC_SMOKE_TUBE_PLATFORM_READY=YES; поставить игрока на пол и подтвердить TC_SMOKE_TUBE_PLAYER_ON_PLATFORM=YES; размещать три блока в том же загруженном чанке на Y=149. Метрика ImageMagick разбирается через awk, без escape-sensitive parameter expansion.
- Offline regression теперь исполняет извлечённые реальные shell-функции с fake compare: ровно 300/20 пикселей принимаются, 299/19 отвергаются; также запрещён escaped metric expansion. Новые изменения только в lane-owned smoke generator, его тесте, evidence verifier и этом журнале. Java, игровые ресурсы, shared client_smoke.sh, registries, Gradle и чужие ветки не изменялись.
- Из client log отдельно обнаружено существующее предупреждение: caster_basic#inventory ищет thaumcraft:entity/armor/caster_basic в block atlas. PNG существует в textures/entity/armor, но item-model/atlas contract требует отдельной диагностики; это следующий самостоятельный M16 пункт после tube smoke.
- Оценка не меняется: 59/100, M16 4/6. Новый точный real-client run обязателен; до его результата ни текстура труб, ни категория не принимаются.

## 10.11.2026 — scan-rune visual false positive rejected; camera-space renderer corrected

- The exact-HEAD real-client workflow [#42751383200](https://github.com/craftkot/tgaumcraft/actions/runs/42751383200) was green, but its visual acceptance is **rejected after screenshot inspection**. The old 300x300 crop included moving sky/clouds. Re-measuring the actual gold block face in screenshots 70/71 and 74/75 (screen crop x=434..589, y=324..479) gives **0 changed pixels on both views**; the old 1295/326 pixel counts came from outside the target face. The render-callback and expiry logs prove particle lifecycle only, not visible output. Do not count this run as scan-rune visual parity.
- Rechecked BETA26 source: `ItemThaumometer.drawFX` calls `FXDispatcher.blockRunes`; that dispatcher adds +0.5 to each supplied coordinate, so the current block/entity spawn anchors were already correct. `FXBlockRunes.renderParticle` performs its Y/Z GL rotations after the camera view matrix is active. The NeoForge `FacingCameraMode` adapter previously rotated an identity quaternion without seeding it from the active camera; it now starts from `camera.rotation()` and applies the original Y quarter-turn then Z 90° tip. Source: [FXBlockRunes.java](https://github.com/TheDarkTower314/Thaumcraft-6-Source-Code/blob/954022bb777b7546281fb36df8522f0ba6b43f81/src/main/java/thaumcraft/client/fx/particles/FXBlockRunes.java), [FXDispatcher.java](https://github.com/TheDarkTower314/Thaumcraft-6-Source-Code/blob/954022bb777b7546281fb36df8522f0ba6b43f81/src/main/java/thaumcraft/client/fx/FXDispatcher.java).
- The smoke now requires a render callback at alpha >= 0.25 and compares only the fixed gold-block face rather than moving clouds. The orientation unit harness now verifies camera-quaternion seeding and the local quarter-turn; its source guard rejects the old sky-inclusive crop. A new exact-HEAD client run is required before accepting visible scan runes.

## 10.11.2026 — scan-rune opposite-face failure traced to camera-space translation

- Exact-HEAD real-client run [#42756472943](https://github.com/craftkot/tgaumcraft/actions/runs/42756472943) on 44b15578 failed its **opposite-view framebuffer check**. The uploaded [artifact #13736211533](https://github.com/craftkot/tgaumcraft/actions/runs/42756472943/artifacts/13736211533) was downloaded and inspected: 80 particles were created/rendered/expired; the first-view target-face diff was 7,053 pixels, but the opposite-view diff was 0. The opposite screenshot showed only narrow side-edge glints, not glyphs on the visible block face. Lifecycle logs alone were not accepted as visual proof.
- Re-read BETA26 FXBlockRunes.renderParticle: its glTranslated(ofx, ofy, -0.51) runs **after** the active camera view matrix, as do the Y quarter-turn and Z 90-degree rotation. The previous port correctly used the camera quaternion for the quad orientation but incorrectly baked the local translation into fixed world axes at particle creation. When the camera turned 180 degrees, the glyph plane rotated with the camera while its 0.51-block offset stayed on the old world face.
- Production fix: retain the unshifted block anchor and local (ofx, ofy, -0.51) vector; on every render transform that vector by Rz(90), the legacy Y quarter-turn, and the current Camera.rotation(). Temporarily apply the result to both current and previous particle positions around super.render in try/finally, then restore the anchor. This also preserves camera movement during the 45-tick lifetime instead of freezing the offset at spawn.
- Added executable orientation-harness checks for the render-time transformed coordinates, 180-degree opposite-camera displacement, current/previous position restoration, and unchanged horizontal drift. The real-client probe now logs camera yaw/look vector/world offset/front-dot and requires front-facing particles from both viewpoints; the framebuffer threshold remains unchanged at 40 pixels.
- Code/test commit: [b38a3c9c7443176b865ad5bcc57f7b73d40c6f26](https://github.com/craftkot/tgaumcraft/commit/b38a3c9c7443176b865ad5bcc57f7b73d40c6f26). A follow-up corrected the source-contract guard to match the actual Java diagnostic expression: [23f72722f928682ccb0bbedd210ac54bb77eb522](https://github.com/craftkot/tgaumcraft/commit/23f72722f928682ccb0bbedd210ac54bb77eb522).
- At journal update time, latest-HEAD GitHub Actions were queued/in progress: real-client push [#42758343090](https://github.com/craftkot/tgaumcraft/actions/runs/42758343090), real-client PR [#42758347906](https://github.com/craftkot/tgaumcraft/actions/runs/42758347906), full CI push [#42758342894](https://github.com/craftkot/tgaumcraft/actions/runs/42758342894), full CI PR [#42758348136](https://github.com/craftkot/tgaumcraft/actions/runs/42758348136). CircleCI verify-connection was pending. No result is claimed until those exact-HEAD jobs finish.
- Fixed audit remains **59/100**, M16 **4/6**. No points awarded: the camera-space renderer correction still requires the two-view real-client framebuffer and full NeoForge acceptance.

### 10.11.2026 — scan-rune client log parser false negative

- Exact-HEAD real Minecraft run #42758541775 completed the world setup and actual particle exercise, then failed with RUNE_ACTUAL_CLIENT_SPAWN_MISSING count=0. The uploaded artifact #13737096351 was downloaded and its client-smoke/client.log inspected directly: 80 CREATED, 80 RENDERED and 80 EXPIRED records are present. The particle constructor, render callback and 45-tick expiry all ran in the actual NeoForge client.
- Root cause is the smoke parser, not particle spawning: its Python raw regex strings contain doubled backslashes (two backslash characters before d), so they match a literal backslash plus d instead of decimal digits. The failure count was a false negative.
- Added a small shared parser with a strict signed/scientific-number grammar and a standalone regression test for all three exact Java log formats, including exponent notation and malformed input. The real-client workflow now runs that parser test before launching Minecraft.
- The artifact's two fixed target-face screenshot pairs were also inspected independently using the script's window geometry (ROOT=1,20; WINDOW=1024x768; crop 155x155 at 435,324). The before/during pixel difference is 0 on the first view and 0 on the opposite view. Thus the render callbacks and the old frontDot (which measures only the position offset) are not visual proof: the scan-rune quads are still not visibly rendered on the target face. The production camera-space change is not accepted.
- Exact-HEAD full CI #42758541792 is SUCCESS: Core parity, source/resource guards, NeoForge build, JAR packaging and dedicated-server smoke. It does not replace the failed graphical client acceptance.
- No production Java code changed in this parser correction. M16 remains 4/6, project index 59/100. Next action after this commit: rerun the real client so it reaches the unchanged framebuffer checks; then diagnose the actual quad orientation/texture mapping from the zero-diff screenshots without weakening thresholds.

### 10.11.2026 — BETA26 scan-rune quad winding and UV parity correction

- The zero-diff client artifact was traced to a second production-side mismatch rather than missing particle creation or blank textures. The pinned BETA26 source at commit 954022bb777b7546281fb36df8522f0ba6b43f81 emits the quad in this exact order: left-top, right-top, right-bottom, left-bottom, with UVs (u1,v1), (u1,v0), (u0,v0), (u0,v1). Its var20 is the full width 0.3*particleScale, so each vertex is at +/-0.5*var20.
- The 1.21.1 SingleQuadParticle implementation emits right-bottom, right-top, left-top, left-bottom, i.e. the opposite winding, and uses the default non-rotated UV assignment. The port inherited that superclass geometry. The front-face screenshot crops were byte-identical before/during (AE=0), consistent with the BETA26-facing side not being emitted with the original winding.
- Replaced the inherited quad emission with a narrow renderRotatedQuad override that writes the exact BETA26 vertex order, UV rotation, full-width scale and fullbright light value. The camera-relative translation and quaternion remain unchanged. Added a real transformed quad-normal diagnostic (quadFacingDot); the smoke now requires both the particle offset and the actual plane normal to face the camera from both directions.
- The isolated Java harness now models the 1.21.1 Camera#setRotation convention (yaw zero rotates local -Z to world +Z) and records actual emitted vertices. It checks vertex count, first-vertex position, all four UVs, front-face normal and fullbright light. This corrects the earlier stub's inconsistent identity quaternion at yaw zero.
- Source comparison: BETA26 FXBlockRunes.java at https://github.com/TheDarkTower314/Thaumcraft-6-Source-Code/blob/954022bb777b7546281fb36df8522f0ba6b43f81/src/main/java/thaumcraft/client/fx/particles/FXBlockRunes.java ; 1.21.1 SingleQuadParticle.java mirror at https://github.com/hackersense/OptiFine-Source/blob/b77c5c6995874f6cf2755bc5234428906b337b75/1.21.1/net/minecraft/client/particle/SingleQuadParticle.java .
- No progress points are awarded yet. Acceptance still requires the exact-HEAD real client to show at least 40 changed target-face pixels on both viewpoints, positive quadFacingDot on both views, 45-tick expiry, and full NeoForge CI. If the new visual test fails, preserve the threshold and inspect the resulting screenshots.

## 2026-11-10 — M16 Salis face-hit fallback now clips the real block outline (CI pending)

Fresh baseline before this iteration:
- `main`: `a4280834adc5a20b6951098af937d30ca4aeca56`.
- Previous lane HEAD: `64594d21d77ac9d9e291b0932f5f0e2f156257b1`.
- All 16 GitHub Actions runs attached to that exact HEAD completed SUCCESS (including full NeoForge CI, dedicated-server startup, Salis real-client dust/particle smoke, crystal outline client smoke, crystal sounds, tube textures, labels/tint, rune particles and GUI/audio checks).
- PR #4 remains Draft/open. PR #1–#3 path checks show no ownership overlap for `SalisMundusSparkles.java`, `tools/test_salis_face_geometry.py`, or the new fallback harness.

Source comparison:
- Pinned BETA26 source: `TheDarkTower314/Thaumcraft-6-Source-Code@954022bb777b7546281fb36df8522f0ba6b43f81`.
- `ItemMagicDust.onItemUseFirst` passes the actual hitX/hitY/hitZ to `doSparkles`; `FXDispatcher.drawBlockSparkles` uses that point to compute distance-based delays.
- The modern caller cannot read those coordinates because its call site `SalisMundusItem.java` is shared with PR #3. Its fallback previously clipped against `new AABB(pos)`, i.e. a full cube. On a slab or multipart block this can produce a hit point outside the actual outline and incorrect face-particle delays.

Implemented:
1. `SalisMundusSparkles.java` now reads the clicked block's `VoxelShape` once. If the authoritative player ray hits the clicked block, it still uses that exact `BlockHitResult`. If not, it uses `shape.clip(eye, eye + look*6, pos)`; only an empty/missed shape falls back to the block center. The later face geometry reuses the same shape. This avoids changing the PR #3-owned item call site.
2. Added `tools/test_salis_hitpoint_fallback.py`. It compiles the actual NeoForge `SalisMundusSparkles.java` against minimal API doubles, forces the player ray to miss the clicked block, supplies a half-height slab outline, and verifies the first UP-face particle has delay 0 at the exact slab top. The old full-cube AABB fallback would yield delay 8, so the fixture distinguishes the regression.
3. Wired this harness into the existing `tools/test_salis_face_geometry.py`, which is already run by `.github/workflows/closure-dust-client.yml`.

Commits:
- `30133681e429ccf9d6ddc7c56211b36f1eca98df` — production shape-aware fallback.
- `2c7cc2c70a713708ee907fb3f51ce4660838232a` — actual-class partial-shape fallback harness.
- `369e7ba1edd3d4bcdbbe2a64108e92e4fecc866f` — hook harness into the existing face-geometry test.

Validation status at HEAD `369e7ba1edd3d4bcdbbe2a64108e92e4fecc866f`:
- GitHub Actions: 17 runs were created for this exact HEAD; all 17 were still QUEUED at 2026-11-10 08:17 CET (no PASS/FAIL yet). Important runs: push full CI #42775639078; push Salis real-client #42775639207; PR Salis real-client #42775646148; PR full CI #42775646120.
- Local execution was attempted via a shallow clone, but the runner cannot resolve github.com (DNS/network unavailable). No local test result is claimed.
- Therefore the new Java stub harness and the real NeoForge/client run remain unverified until Actions starts. Do not count this as accepted or award M16/overall points yet.

Next action: inspect #42775639207 and #42775646148 first. If the harness fails, fix that before other work. 5. The native test uses a test-only 1200-tick face delay to leave enough time for unload; production BETA26 delay rules are unchanged.

## 2026-11-10 — restore BETA26 crystal support-material parity

Source comparison:
- Pinned BETA26 `BlockCrystal#drawAt` accepts a neighboring block only when `getMaterial() == Material.ROCK` and that exact face is `isSideSolid`.
- The port previously used `BASE_STONE_OVERWORLD` plus only cobblestone/deepslate/stone bricks. This rejected other BETA26 rock supports such as sandstone and nether bricks, despite their 1.12 block classes using `Material.ROCK`.
- Sources: [BETA26 BlockCrystal](https://github.com/TheDarkTower314/Thaumcraft-6-Source-Code/blob/954022bb/src/main/java/thaumcraft/common/blocks/world/ore/BlockCrystal.java), [1.12 BlockSandStone](https://github.com/Wharaden/MCP-1.12/blob/master/Desktop/MCP%201.12/src/minecraft/net/minecraft/block/BlockSandStone.java), [1.12 BlockNetherBrick](https://github.com/Wharaden/MCP-1.12/blob/master/Desktop/MCP%201.12/src/minecraft/net/minecraft/block/BlockNetherBrick.java).

Change:
- Replaced the narrow hard-coded predicate with the data-driven `thaumcraft:crystal_supports` block tag. The existing per-face `isFaceSturdy` gate remains, so a tagged stair/fence does not become a support on faces that are not actually sturdy.
- Added the vanilla 1.12-era rock-block counterparts, retaining the modern base-stone set and allowing mod/datapack extensions without another Java registry.
- Extended the real Minecraft crystal-outline smoke: north-face support now uses sandstone and south-face support uses nether bricks; all six face ray-picking cases still run. The script requires both material markers and the full client ray-pick acceptance marker.

Changed files:
- `src/neoforge/java/dev/ehtid/thaumcraft/block/CrystalClusterBlock.java`
- `src/main/resources/data/thaumcraft/tags/block/crystal_supports.json` (new)
- `tools/client_crystal_outline_smoke.py`
- `tools/test_crystal_outline_smoke.py`
- this lane journal.

Exact-head validation is pending. Required: crystal-outline source contract, generated Bash syntax, full NeoForge CI/data-pack load, and actual Minecraft client `CRYSTAL_OUTLINE_LEGACY_ROCK_SUPPORTS=PASS` plus `CRYSTAL_OUTLINE_REAL_CLIENT_RAY_PICK=PASS`. Do not infer success from the earlier stone-only smoke [#42797632305](https://github.com/craftkot/tgaumcraft/actions/runs/42797632305).

The fixed project audit remains 59/100; M16 remains 4/6 and M08 remains 5/9 until the broader criteria are re-audited. No points awarded for this source change or a compile alone. PR #4 remains Draft/open; no merge.


### 2026-11-10 follow-up — correct the crystal smoke generator before acceptance

The first support-tag commit `927af74` exposed a generator-editing error: JavaScript replacement-string expansion interpreted the shell regex token `$'` and duplicated the tail of the embedded Bash template. The real-client workflow [#42823583393](https://github.com/craftkot/tgaumcraft/actions/runs/42823583393) failed before Minecraft launch; this was a test-generator failure, not a crystal runtime result. Rebuilt `client_crystal_outline_smoke.py` from its clean parent version using literal slice replacement, then reapplied the support-material cases and acceptance markers. The corrected file is checked to end at its single Python entry point; the rerun on the fix commit is authoritative.


## 2026-11-10 — M16 real-client evidence and tube-smoke false-positive blocker

Current tested feature head before this fix: 371d9b37e80a4f0eacda0fbc2a76a685a8cc7cdc (PR #4 base main at a4280834adc5a20b6951098af937d30ca4aeca56). The fresh audit on main still scores the whole port 59/100; the audited category is Модели, звуки, частицы, русская локализация, полировка интерфейса: 4/6. No point is awarded by this entry.

Accepted real-client evidence on that exact feature head:

- Curio learn sound, run #42824117075: PASS. Original-source PCM correlation 0.9741, baseline correlation 0.0476; the independent BETA26 audible-pitch control reports requested values clamped to 0.50000 and real PCM 0.50000. Background music/ambient isolation and OpenAL/Pulse routing checks passed.
- Thaumonomicon page sound/GUI, run #42824117271: PASS. Page-2 source waveform correlation 1.0000; real page-sound dispatch/resolution count 1 and GUI framebuffer difference 83200 pixels.
- Crystal break sound, run #42824117382: PASS. Actual block break and original PCM correlation 0.7474, baseline 0.0121, pitch 0.8000; before/after screenshots show the crystal removed.
- Six crystal variants placement and sound, run #42824117061: PASS for aer/ignis/terra/ordo/perditio/vitium. Per-variant source correlations: 0.8135/0.5857/0.5222/0.6048/0.8353/0.8310, all with baseline below 0.014, original pitch 0.8000.
- Thaumometer rune real client, run #42824117096: PASS. Both opposite camera orientations, 80 spawned/rendered rune particles, visible alpha 0.278, actual framebuffer differences 4015 and 2699, and expiry confirmed. The captured before/during/after screenshots were inspected; rune visible during use and absent after expiry.
- Salis original dust, run #42824117151: PASS. Real bookshelf gameplay, original dust PCM correlation 0.6469 (baseline 0.0000, pitch 1.0390), native chunk unload/reload/ticket cleanup, 16-frame 320/512 sprite animation; early warm pixels 5882, late 0. Screenshots were inspected. The pixel gate itself explicitly does not claim byte-for-byte BETA26 sprite parity.
- Full port CI, run #42824117292: PASS (Core/NeoForge/packaging pipeline).

Tube real-client run #42824117218 FAILED in its final evidence verifier, despite the preceding full-frame pixel/particle checks reporting PASS. Artifact #13769820049 was inspected. Its 71-tubes-connected.png is the Creative inventory/search screen, not the world view; the search field contains the tail of a typed tube command. The client log records only two of three /setblock commands and no valve/middle/restrict connection markers. Therefore TUBE_BLOCK_RENDER_DIFF=765952 and particle deltas 7150/7641 are invalid UI-driven false positives and are not accepted as tube visual/particle evidence. TUBE_NO_MISSING_MODEL_TEXTURES=PASS does independently show that the tube/base_metal models had no missing-texture warning in this run.

Root cause: the tube harness used a custom tube_send_chat_quick with 150 ms to open chat, 2 ms per typed character and only 120 ms after Enter. The shared client harness's already-proven send_chat uses 1 s to open, 24 ms per character and 4 s for server response. The tube script raced UI focus, causing later command text to enter the Creative inventory search. This is a test-harness defect, not yet a production tube-connection defect.

This commit replaces the quick path with a deterministic chat helper using the proven 1 s/24 ms timings, but waits for the expected server-log acknowledgement (up to 4 s) rather than a fixed 4 s sleep so destruction-particle screenshots are captured while the effect is still alive. Every setup, placement, connection and destruction command must be acknowledged before the script continues. The verifier additionally requires three real block-placement acknowledgements and explicit valve/restrict air-state markers after destruction. Existing pixel/particle thresholds are unchanged. Acceptance for this corrected harness is pending the new workflow run; do not accept the tube category until the new artifact shows the actual tube world (not an inventory screen), all three blockstate markers, destruction markers, and valid particle frames.

Accepted current-head evidence is limited to the runs listed above. Still running on the prior head when this entry was written: crystal outline/picking #42824117144, Thaumometer scan sound #42824117305, and crystal placement sound #42824117511. M16 remains 4/6 pending those results and the corrected tube smoke; next step is to inspect these workflows and the new tube run before choosing another M16 defect.
